**Circular Number:** CIR/2027/163
**Date:** 03 August 2027
**Society Name:** Banyan Tree Residences
**Issued By:** Nisha Kapoor (Secretary)

**Subject:** Noise Pollution Control - Quiet Hours Policy

**To All Members,**

This circular is issued to inform all residents about the noise restrictions that have been approved by the Managing Committee and are hereby notified for strict compliance.

**Detailed Explanation:**

The society has received 40 written complaints in the last 5 months regarding noise disturbances during late hours. The Managing Committee is duty-bound to ensure peaceful living environment for all residents.

As per Noise Pollution (Regulation and Control) Rules, 2000, residential areas are classified as 'Silence Zone' and permissible noise levels must be maintained. Repeated violations may lead to police complaints.

**Applicable Rules:**

1. Quiet hours: 10:30 PM to 6:30 AM and 2:00 PM to 4:00 PM
2. No drilling, hammering, or construction noise during quiet hours
3. Music and TV volume to be kept at reasonable levels
4. Parties must end by 11:00 PM on weekdays, 12:00 AM on weekends
5. Generator usage restricted to emergencies only
6. Washing machine usage discouraged after 10:00 PM

**Effective Date:** 23 August 2027

**Penalty for Non-Compliance:**
First Violation: Written warning
Second Violation: Fine of ₹2000
Repeated Violations: Matter referred to Managing Committee for further action

**Implementation:**
- All residents must acknowledge receipt of this circular
- Tenants to be informed by flat owners
- Copy displayed on all notice boards
- Soft copy available on society app/website

For any clarifications, please contact the society office during working hours (10:00 AM to 1:00 PM and 4:00 PM to 7:00 PM).

**Issued By:**
Nisha Kapoor (Secretary)
Banyan Tree Residences

**Contact:** office@banyantreeresidences.com
**Phone:** +91-9980767214

**Society Name:** Vasant Vihar Apartments
**Issued By:** Chairperson

**Subject:** Timely Payment of Maintenance Charges - Penalty Notification

**To All Members,**

This circular is issued to inform all residents about the maintenance payment reminders that have been approved by the Managing Committee and are hereby notified for strict compliance.

**Detailed Explanation:**

As of May 2025, the society has outstanding dues of ₹581,519 from 52 defaulting members. This affects the society's ability to maintain essential services and undertake repairs.

The committee has exhausted all informal reminders and is now compelled to take legal action. Members are urged to clear pending dues immediately to avoid legal complications and damage to their credit record.

**Applicable Rules:**

1. Maintenance due by 10th of every month
2. Late payment interest: 18% per annum after 15th
3. Defaulters beyond 60 days barred from amenities
4. Legal notice after 90 days non-payment
5. Names of chronic defaulters displayed on notice board
6. Society can initiate recovery under Section 101 of MCS Act

**Effective Date:** 17 May 2025

**Penalty for Non-Compliance:**
First Violation: Written warning
Second Violation: Fine of ₹1000
Repeated Violations: Matter referred to Managing Committee for further action

**Implementation:**
- All residents must acknowledge receipt of this circular
- Tenants to be informed by flat owners
- Copy displayed on all notice boards
- Soft copy available on society app/website

**Circular Number:** CIR/2024/062
**Date:** 17 June 2024
**Society Name:** Sunrise Enclave
**Issued By:** Managing Committee

**Subject:** Water Conservation Initiative - Usage Guidelines

**To All Members,**

This circular is issued to inform all residents about the water conservation measures that have been approved by the Managing Committee and are hereby notified for strict compliance.

**Detailed Explanation:**

The society's daily water consumption has increased to 41982 liters. With depleting groundwater levels and erratic municipal supply, water conservation is no longer optional but essential.

The society has installed flow meters and leak detection systems. Residents consuming excessive water (above 642 liters per day per flat) will be identified and counseled.

**Applicable Rules:**

1. Car washing limited to two buckets - no hose pipes
2. Washing timings: 6:00 AM to 10:00 AM only
3. Report leaking taps and pipes immediately
4. Rainwater harvesting system installed - cooperation required
5. Overhead tank cleaning every 3 months
6. Penalty for water wastage: ₹1,000 per incident

**Effective Date:** 05 July 2024

**Penalty for Non-Compliance:**
First Violation: Written warning
Second Violation: Fine of ₹5000
Repeated Violations: Matter referred to Managing Committee for further action

**Implementation:**
- All residents must acknowledge receipt of this circular
- Tenants to be informed by flat owners
- Copy displayed on all notice boards
- Soft copy available on society app/website

For any clarifications, please contact the society office during working hours (10:00 AM to 1:00 PM and 4:00 PM to 7:00 PM).

**Circular Number:** CIR/2027/158
**Date:** 08 April 2027
**Society Name:** Blue Horizons CHS
**Issued By:** Secretary

**Subject:** Fire Safety Measures - Mandatory Compliance Requirements

**To All Members,**

This circular is issued to inform all residents about the fire safety compliance that have been approved by the Managing Committee and are hereby notified for strict compliance.

**Detailed Explanation:**

As per Maharashtra Fire Prevention and Life Safety Measures Act, 2006, and recent directives from the Fire Department, all residential societies must comply with specified fire safety norms. The last fire audit identified 8 areas requiring immediate attention.

The society has appointed Phoenix Fire Systems as authorized fire safety consultants. Non-compliance may result in penalties from municipal authorities.

**Applicable Rules:**

1. Fire extinguishers must be recharged annually
2. Fire doors to be kept closed (not locked) at all times
3. No storage of combustible materials in staircases
4. Smoke detectors must be functional - testing every 6 months
5. Fire drill participation mandatory twice a year
6. Emergency exit routes must be unobstructed

**Effective Date:** 28 April 2027

**Penalty for Non-Compliance:**
First Violation: Written warning
Second Violation: Fine of ₹5000
Repeated Violations: Matter referred to Managing Committee for further action

**Implementation:**
- All residents must acknowledge receipt of this circular
- Tenants to be informed by flat owners
- Copy displayed on all notice boards
- Soft copy available on society app/website

**Date:** 23 August 2025
**Society Name:** Sapphire Heights Cooperative Housing Society
**Issued By:** Managing Committee

**Subject:** Mandatory Registration of Pets and Animal Welfare Guidelines

**To All Members,**

This circular is issued to inform all residents about the pet registration requirement that have been approved by the Managing Committee and are hereby notified for strict compliance.

**Detailed Explanation:**

As per Animal Welfare Board of India (AWBI) guidelines and judgment of the Honorable Supreme Court, the society cannot ban pets. However, to ensure harmonious coexistence and address concerns of non-pet owners, the following registration and conduct guidelines are being implemented.

Currently, approximately 39 pet dogs and 16 cats reside in our society. Proper registration will help maintain records and ensure responsible pet ownership.

**Applicable Rules:**

1. All pet dogs and cats must be registered with society office
2. Annual vaccination certificate to be submitted
3. Pets must be on leash (max 6 feet) in common areas
4. Pet waste must be cleaned immediately by owners
5. Aggressive pets must be muzzled in public areas
6. Lift usage allowed but give preference to seniors

**Effective Date:** 20 September 2025

**Penalty for Non-Compliance:**
First Violation: Written warning
Second Violation: Fine of ₹1000
Repeated Violations: Matter referred to Managing Committee for further action

**Implementation:**
- All residents must acknowledge receipt of this circular
- Tenants to be informed by flat owners
- Copy displayed on all notice boards
- Soft copy available on society app/website

For any clarifications, please contact the society office during working hours (10:00 AM to 1:00 PM and 4:00 PM to 7:00 PM).

**Society Name:** Silver Oaks Apartment Owners Association
**Issued By:** Seema Joshi (Secretary)

**Subject:** Guidelines for Internal Renovation and Structural Work

**To All Members,**

This circular is issued to inform all residents about the renovation approval process that have been approved by the Managing Committee and are hereby notified for strict compliance.

**Detailed Explanation:**

To prevent unauthorized structural modifications that may compromise building safety and to minimize inconvenience to other residents, the society has formulated a comprehensive renovation approval process.

The society engineer will inspect the work site before and after renovation. Any structural damage to common areas or neighboring flats will result in forfeiture of the security deposit and additional penalty.

**Applicable Rules:**

1. NOC required from society for any structural changes
2. Refundable deposit: ₹25,000 before starting work
3. Permitted hours: 9:00 AM to 5:00 PM, Monday to Saturday
4. No work on Sundays and public holidays
5. Debris must be removed within 24 hours
6. Load-bearing walls cannot be altered under any circumstance

**Effective Date:** 23 August 2026

**Penalty for Non-Compliance:**
First Violation: Written warning
Second Violation: Fine of ₹2000
Repeated Violations: Matter referred to Managing Committee for further action

**Implementation:**
- All residents must acknowledge receipt of this circular
- Tenants to be informed by flat owners
- Copy displayed on all notice boards

In view of recent security incidents in neighboring societies and to enhance the safety of all residents, the Managing Committee has decided to implement a comprehensive visitor management system.

**Circular Number:** CIR/2027/158
**Date:** 17 June 2027
**Society Name:** Royal Enclave CHS
**Issued By:** Chairperson

**Subject:** Timely Payment of Maintenance Charges - Penalty Notification

**To All Members,**

This circular is issued to inform all residents about the maintenance payment reminders that have been approved by the Managing Committee and are hereby notified for strict compliance.

**Detailed Explanation:**

As of June 2027, the society has outstanding dues of ₹1,390,651 from 53 defaulting members. This affects the society's ability to maintain essential services and undertake repairs.

The committee has exhausted all informal reminders and is now compelled to take legal action. Members are urged to clear pending dues immediately to avoid legal complications and damage to their credit record.

**Applicable Rules:**

1. Maintenance due by 10th of every month
2. Late payment interest: 18% per annum after 15th
3. Defaulters beyond 60 days barred from amenities
4. Legal notice after 90 days non-payment
5. Names of chronic defaulters displayed on notice board
6. Society can initiate recovery under Section 101 of MCS Act

**Effective Date:** 13 July 2027

**Penalty for Non-Compliance:**
First Violation: Written warning
Second Violation: Fine of ₹5000
Repeated Violations: Matter referred to Managing Committee for further action

**Implementation:**
- All residents must acknowledge receipt of this circular
- Tenants to be informed by flat owners
- Copy displayed on all notice boards
- Soft copy available on society app/website

**Issued By:** Secretary

**Subject:** Water Conservation Initiative - Usage Guidelines

**To All Members,**

This circular is issued to inform all residents about the water conservation measures that have been approved by the Managing Committee and are hereby notified for strict compliance.

**Detailed Explanation:**

The society's daily water consumption has increased to 76850 liters. With depleting groundwater levels and erratic municipal supply, water conservation is no longer optional but essential.

The society has installed flow meters and leak detection systems. Residents consuming excessive water (above 738 liters per day per flat) will be identified and counseled.

**Applicable Rules:**

1. Car washing limited to two buckets - no hose pipes
2. Washing timings: 6:00 AM to 10:00 AM only
3. Report leaking taps and pipes immediately
4. Rainwater harvesting system installed - cooperation required
5. Overhead tank cleaning every 3 months
6. Penalty for water wastage: ₹1,000 per incident

**Effective Date:** 20 August 2025

**Penalty for Non-Compliance:**
First Violation: Written warning
Second Violation: Fine of ₹1000
Repeated Violations: Matter referred to Managing Committee for further action

**Implementation:**
- All residents must acknowledge receipt of this circular
- Tenants to be informed by flat owners
- Copy displayed on all notice boards
- Soft copy available on society app/website

**Society Name:** Silver Springs
**Issued By:** Managing Committee

**Subject:** Fire Safety Measures - Mandatory Compliance Requirements

**To All Members,**

This circular is issued to inform all residents about the fire safety compliance that have been approved by the Managing Committee and are hereby notified for strict compliance.

**Detailed Explanation:**

As per Maharashtra Fire Prevention and Life Safety Measures Act, 2006, and recent directives from the Fire Department, all residential societies must comply with specified fire safety norms. The last fire audit identified 15 areas requiring immediate attention.

The society has appointed SecureFire India as authorized fire safety consultants. Non-compliance may result in penalties from municipal authorities.

**Applicable Rules:**

1. Fire extinguishers must be recharged annually
2. Fire doors to be kept closed (not locked) at all times
3. No storage of combustible materials in staircases
4. Smoke detectors must be functional - testing every 6 months
5. Fire drill participation mandatory twice a year
6. Emergency exit routes must be unobstructed

**Effective Date:** 28 May 2025

**Penalty for Non-Compliance:**
First Violation: Written warning
Second Violation: Fine of ₹500
Repeated Violations: Matter referred to Managing Committee for further action

**Implementation:**
- All residents must acknowledge receipt of this circular
- Tenants to be informed by flat owners
- Copy displayed on all notice boards
- Soft copy available on society app/website

**Subject:** Guidelines for Internal Renovation and Structural Work

**To All Members,**

This circular is issued to inform all residents about the renovation approval process that have been approved by the Managing Committee and are hereby notified for strict compliance.

**Detailed Explanation:**

To prevent unauthorized structural modifications that may compromise building safety and to minimize inconvenience to other residents, the society has formulated a comprehensive renovation approval process.

The society engineer will inspect the work site before and after renovation. Any structural damage to common areas or neighboring flats will result in forfeiture of the security deposit and additional penalty.

**Applicable Rules:**

1. NOC required from society for any structural changes
2. Refundable deposit: ₹25,000 before starting work
3. Permitted hours: 9:00 AM to 5:00 PM, Monday to Saturday
4. No work on Sundays and public holidays
5. Debris must be removed within 24 hours
6. Load-bearing walls cannot be altered under any circumstance

**Effective Date:** 25 January 2026

**Penalty for Non-Compliance:**
First Violation: Written warning
Second Violation: Fine of ₹500
Repeated Violations: Matter referred to Managing Committee for further action

**Subject:** Mandatory Registration of Pets and Animal Welfare Guidelines

**To All Members,**

This circular is issued to inform all residents about the pet registration requirement that have been approved by the Managing Committee and are hereby notified for strict compliance.

**Detailed Explanation:**

As per Animal Welfare Board of India (AWBI) guidelines and judgment of the Honorable Supreme Court, the society cannot ban pets. However, to ensure harmonious coexistence and address concerns of non-pet owners, the following registration and conduct guidelines are being implemented.

Currently, approximately 66 pet dogs and 24 cats reside in our society. Proper registration will help maintain records and ensure responsible pet ownership.

**Applicable Rules:**

1. All pet dogs and cats must be registered with society office
2. Annual vaccination certificate to be submitted
3. Pets must be on leash (max 6 feet) in common areas
4. Pet waste must be cleaned immediately by owners
5. Aggressive pets must be muzzled in public areas
6. Lift usage allowed but give preference to seniors

**Effective Date:** 24 July 2025

**Penalty for Non-Compliance:**
First Violation: Written warning
Second Violation: Fine of ₹1000
Repeated Violations: Matter referred to Managing Committee for further action

- All residents must acknowledge receipt of this circular
- Tenants to be informed by flat owners

**Circular Number:** CIR/2024/086
**Date:** 12 July 2024
**Society Name:** Cedar Crest Society
**Issued By:** Managing Committee

**Subject:** Water Conservation Initiative - Usage Guidelines

**To All Members,**

This circular is issued to inform all residents about the water conservation measures that have been approved by the Managing Committee and are hereby notified for strict compliance.

**Detailed Explanation:**

The society's daily water consumption has increased to 43470 liters. With depleting groundwater levels and erratic municipal supply, water conservation is no longer optional but essential.

The society has installed flow meters and leak detection systems. Residents consuming excessive water (above 538 liters per day per flat) will be identified and counseled.

**Applicable Rules:**

1. Car washing limited to two buckets - no hose pipes
2. Washing timings: 6:00 AM to 10:00 AM only
3. Report leaking taps and pipes immediately
4. Rainwater harvesting system installed - cooperation required
5. Overhead tank cleaning every 3 months
6. Penalty for water wastage: ₹1,000 per incident

**Effective Date:** 10 August 2024

**Penalty for Non-Compliance:**
First Violation: Written warning
Second Violation: Fine of ₹2000
Repeated Violations: Matter referred to Managing Committee for further action

**Implementation:**
- All residents must acknowledge receipt of this circular
- Tenants to be informed by flat owners
- Copy displayed on all notice boards
- Soft copy available on society app/website

For any clarifications, please contact the society office during working hours (10:00 AM to 1:00 PM and 4:00 PM to 7:00 PM).

**Issued By:** Managing Committee

**Subject:** Noise Pollution Control - Quiet Hours Policy

**To All Members,**

This circular is issued to inform all residents about the noise restrictions that have been approved by the Managing Committee and are hereby notified for strict compliance.

**Detailed Explanation:**

The society has received 18 written complaints in the last 6 months regarding noise disturbances during late hours. The Managing Committee is duty-bound to ensure peaceful living environment for all residents.

As per Noise Pollution (Regulation and Control) Rules, 2000, residential areas are classified as 'Silence Zone' and permissible noise levels must be maintained. Repeated violations may lead to police complaints.

**Applicable Rules:**

1. Quiet hours: 10:30 PM to 6:30 AM and 2:00 PM to 4:00 PM
2. No drilling, hammering, or construction noise during quiet hours
3. Music and TV volume to be kept at reasonable levels
4. Parties must end by 11:00 PM on weekdays, 12:00 AM on weekends
5. Generator usage restricted to emergencies only
6. Washing machine usage discouraged after 10:00 PM

**Effective Date:** 11 May 2024

**Penalty for Non-Compliance:**
First Violation: Written warning
Second Violation: Fine of ₹1000
Repeated Violations: Matter referred to Managing Committee for further action

**Implementation:**
- All residents must acknowledge receipt of this circular
- Tenants to be informed by flat owners
- Copy displayed on all notice boards
- Soft copy available on society app/website

For any clarifications, please contact the society office during working hours (10:00 AM to 1:00 PM and 4:00 PM to 7:00 PM).

**Circular Number:** CIR/2024/114
**Date:** 24 January 2024
**Society Name:** Ruby Residency
**Issued By:** Managing Committee

**Subject:** Guidelines for Internal Renovation and Structural Work

**To All Members,**

This circular is issued to inform all residents about the renovation approval process that have been approved by the Managing Committee and are hereby notified for strict compliance.

**Detailed Explanation:**

To prevent unauthorized structural modifications that may compromise building safety and to minimize inconvenience to other residents, the society has formulated a comprehensive renovation approval process.

The society engineer will inspect the work site before and after renovation. Any structural damage to common areas or neighboring flats will result in forfeiture of the security deposit and additional penalty.

**Applicable Rules:**

1. NOC required from society for any structural changes
2. Refundable deposit: ₹25,000 before starting work
3. Permitted hours: 9:00 AM to 5:00 PM, Monday to Saturday
4. No work on Sundays and public holidays
5. Debris must be removed within 24 hours
6. Load-bearing walls cannot be altered under any circumstance

**Subject:** Mandatory Registration of Pets and Animal Welfare Guidelines

**To All Members,**

This circular is issued to inform all residents about the pet registration requirement that have been approved by the Managing Committee and are hereby notified for strict compliance.

**Detailed Explanation:**

As per Animal Welfare Board of India (AWBI) guidelines and judgment of the Honorable Supreme Court, the society cannot ban pets. However, to ensure harmonious coexistence and address concerns of non-pet owners, the following registration and conduct guidelines are being implemented.

Currently, approximately 55 pet dogs and 38 cats reside in our society. Proper registration will help maintain records and ensure responsible pet ownership.

**Applicable Rules:**

1. All pet dogs and cats must be registered with society office
2. Annual vaccination certificate to be submitted
3. Pets must be on leash (max 6 feet) in common areas
4. Pet waste must be cleaned immediately by owners
5. Aggressive pets must be muzzled in public areas
6. Lift usage allowed but give preference to seniors

**Effective Date:** 15 July 2026

**Penalty for Non-Compliance:**
First Violation: Written warning
Second Violation: Fine of ₹1000
Repeated Violations: Matter referred to Managing Committee for further action

**Implementation:**

- Tenants to be informed by flat owners

**Subject:** Fire Safety Measures - Mandatory Compliance Requirements

**To All Members,**

This circular is issued to inform all residents about the fire safety compliance that have been approved by the Managing Committee and are hereby notified for strict compliance.

**Detailed Explanation:**

As per Maharashtra Fire Prevention and Life Safety Measures Act, 2006, and recent directives from the Fire Department, all residential societies must comply with specified fire safety norms. The last fire audit identified 5 areas requiring immediate attention.

The society has appointed Phoenix Fire Systems as authorized fire safety consultants. Non-compliance may result in penalties from municipal authorities.

**Applicable Rules:**

1. Fire extinguishers must be recharged annually
2. Fire doors to be kept closed (not locked) at all times
3. No storage of combustible materials in staircases
4. Smoke detectors must be functional - testing every 6 months
5. Fire drill participation mandatory twice a year
6. Emergency exit routes must be unobstructed

**Effective Date:** 01 October 2025

**Penalty for Non-Compliance:**
First Violation: Written warning
Second Violation: Fine of ₹500
Repeated Violations: Matter referred to Managing Committee for further action

**To All Members,**

This circular is issued to inform all residents about the maintenance payment reminders that have been approved by the Managing Committee and are hereby notified for strict compliance.

**Date:** 29 March 2027
**Society Name:** Orchid Petals Society
**Issued By:** Komal Das (Secretary)

**Subject:** Noise Pollution Control - Quiet Hours Policy

**To All Members,**

This circular is issued to inform all residents about the noise restrictions that have been approved by the Managing Committee and are hereby notified for strict compliance.

**Detailed Explanation:**

The society has received 32 written complaints in the last 4 months regarding noise disturbances during late hours. The Managing Committee is duty-bound to ensure peaceful living environment for all residents.

As per Noise Pollution (Regulation and Control) Rules, 2000, residential areas are classified as 'Silence Zone' and permissible noise levels must be maintained. Repeated violations may lead to police complaints.

**Applicable Rules:**

1. Quiet hours: 10:30 PM to 6:30 AM and 2:00 PM to 4:00 PM
2. No drilling, hammering, or construction noise during quiet hours
3. Music and TV volume to be kept at reasonable levels
4. Parties must end by 11:00 PM on weekdays, 12:00 AM on weekends
5. Generator usage restricted to emergencies only
6. Washing machine usage discouraged after 10:00 PM

**Effective Date:** 12 April 2027

**Penalty for Non-Compliance:**
First Violation: Written warning
Second Violation: Fine of ₹2000
Repeated Violations: Matter referred to Managing Committee for further action

**Implementation:**
- All residents must acknowledge receipt of this circular
- Tenants to be informed by flat owners
- Copy displayed on all notice boards
- Soft copy available on society app/website

**Issued By:** Secretary

**Subject:** Water Conservation Initiative - Usage Guidelines

**To All Members,**

This circular is issued to inform all residents about the water conservation measures that have been approved by the Managing Committee and are hereby notified for strict compliance.

**Detailed Explanation:**

The society's daily water consumption has increased to 69394 liters. With depleting groundwater levels and erratic municipal supply, water conservation is no longer optional but essential.

The society has installed flow meters and leak detection systems. Residents consuming excessive water (above 798 liters per day per flat) will be identified and counseled.

**Applicable Rules:**

1. Car washing limited to two buckets - no hose pipes
2. Washing timings: 6:00 AM to 10:00 AM only
3. Report leaking taps and pipes immediately
4. Rainwater harvesting system installed - cooperation required
5. Overhead tank cleaning every 3 months
6. Penalty for water wastage: ₹1,000 per incident

**Effective Date:** 01 March 2025

**Penalty for Non-Compliance:**
First Violation: Written warning
Second Violation: Fine of ₹1000
Repeated Violations: Matter referred to Managing Committee for further action

**Subject:** Water Conservation Initiative - Usage Guidelines

**To All Members,**

This circular is issued to inform all residents about the water conservation measures that have been approved by the Managing Committee and are hereby notified for strict compliance.

**Detailed Explanation:**

The society's daily water consumption has increased to 38941 liters. With depleting groundwater levels and erratic municipal supply, water conservation is no longer optional but essential.

The society has installed flow meters and leak detection systems. Residents consuming excessive water (above 613 liters per day per flat) will be identified and counseled.

**Applicable Rules:**

1. Car washing limited to two buckets - no hose pipes
2. Washing timings: 6:00 AM to 10:00 AM only
3. Report leaking taps and pipes immediately
4. Rainwater harvesting system installed - cooperation required
5. Overhead tank cleaning every 3 months
6. Penalty for water wastage: ₹1,000 per incident

**Effective Date:** 09 September 2026

**Penalty for Non-Compliance:**
First Violation: Written warning
Second Violation: Fine of ₹500
Repeated Violations: Matter referred to Managing Committee for further action

**Implementation:**
- All residents must acknowledge receipt of this circular
- Tenants to be informed by flat owners
- Copy displayed on all notice boards
- Soft copy available on society app/website

For any clarifications, please contact the society office during working hours (10:00 AM to 1:00 PM and 4:00 PM to 7:00 PM).

**Issued By:**
Chairperson
Paradise Heights

**Contact:** office@paradiseheights.com
**Phone:** +91-7386426437

**Circular Number:** CIR/2025/161
**Date:** 28 May 2025
**Society Name:** Eco-Village CHS
**Issued By:** Estate Manager

**Subject:** Revised Parking Policy and Slot Allocation Guidelines

**To All Members,**

This circular is issued to inform all residents about the new parking rules that have been approved by the Managing Committee and are hereby notified for strict compliance.

**Detailed Explanation:**

The society has 192 parking slots for 275 flats. Due to increasing complaints about unauthorized parking and space shortage, the committee has revised the parking policy. This policy aims to ensure fair allocation and systematic management of limited parking resources.

The new parking rules have been formulated after consultation with 21 resident representatives and legal advisors. These rules are binding on all residents, tenants, and their guests.

**Applicable Rules:**

1. One parking slot per flat as per original allotment deed
2. Second parking available on rental basis - ₹1,500 per month
3. Guest parking limited to 6 hours - overstay fine ₹500
4. No parking in fire lane - violators will be towed
5. Visitor vehicles must display dashboard pass
6. Two-wheeler parking only in designated zones

**Effective Date:** 04 June 2025

**Penalty for Non-Compliance:**
First Violation: Written warning
Second Violation: Fine of ₹2000
Repeated Violations: Matter referred to Managing Committee for further action

**Implementation:**
- All residents must acknowledge receipt of this circular
- Tenants to be informed by flat owners
- Copy displayed on all notice boards
- Soft copy available on society app/website

For any clarifications, please contact the society office during working hours (10:00 AM to 1:00 PM and 4:00 PM to 7:00 PM).

**Date:** 19 October 2027
**Society Name:** Blue Horizons CHS
**Issued By:** Chairperson

**Subject:** Fire Safety Measures - Mandatory Compliance Requirements

**To All Members,**

This circular is issued to inform all residents about the fire safety compliance that have been approved by the Managing Committee and are hereby notified for strict compliance.

**Detailed Explanation:**

As per Maharashtra Fire Prevention and Life Safety Measures Act, 2006, and recent directives from the Fire Department, all residential societies must comply with specified fire safety norms. The last fire audit identified 5 areas requiring immediate attention.

The society has appointed M/s Fire Safe Solutions as authorized fire safety consultants. Non-compliance may result in penalties from municipal authorities.

**Applicable Rules:**

1. Fire extinguishers must be recharged annually
2. Fire doors to be kept closed (not locked) at all times
3. No storage of combustible materials in staircases
4. Smoke detectors must be functional - testing every 6 months
5. Fire drill participation mandatory twice a year
6. Emergency exit routes must be unobstructed

**Effective Date:** 15 November 2027

**Penalty for Non-Compliance:**
First Violation: Written warning
Second Violation: Fine of ₹500
Repeated Violations: Matter referred to Managing Committee for further action

**Implementation:**
- All residents must acknowledge receipt of this circular
- Tenants to be informed by flat owners
- Copy displayed on all notice boards
- Soft copy available on society app/website

The society has invested ₹705,750 in digital gate management infrastructure including facial recognition cameras and automated entry systems. All residents are requested to cooperate with security personnel.

**Applicable Rules:**

3. Delivery executives restricted to lobby after 10 PM

6. Frequent visitors (tutors, nurses) must have monthly pass

**Circular Number:** CIR/2025/163
**Date:** 16 March 2025
**Society Name:** Pearl Residency
**Issued By:** Nitin Iyer (Secretary)

**Subject:** Mandatory Registration of Pets and Animal Welfare Guidelines

**To All Members,**

This circular is issued to inform all residents about the pet registration requirement that have been approved by the Managing Committee and are hereby notified for strict compliance.

**Detailed Explanation:**

As per Animal Welfare Board of India (AWBI) guidelines and judgment of the Honorable Supreme Court, the society cannot ban pets. However, to ensure harmonious coexistence and address concerns of non-pet owners, the following registration and conduct guidelines are being implemented.

Currently, approximately 27 pet dogs and 16 cats reside in our society. Proper registration will help maintain records and ensure responsible pet ownership.

**Applicable Rules:**

1. All pet dogs and cats must be registered with society office
2. Annual vaccination certificate to be submitted
3. Pets must be on leash (max 6 feet) in common areas
4. Pet waste must be cleaned immediately by owners
5. Aggressive pets must be muzzled in public areas
6. Lift usage allowed but give preference to seniors

**Effective Date:** 12 April 2025

**Penalty for Non-Compliance:**
First Violation: Written warning
Second Violation: Fine of ₹500
Repeated Violations: Matter referred to Managing Committee for further action

**Implementation:**
- All residents must acknowledge receipt of this circular
- Tenants to be informed by flat owners
- Copy displayed on all notice boards
- Soft copy available on society app/website

For any clarifications, please contact the society office during working hours (10:00 AM to 1:00 PM and 4:00 PM to 7:00 PM).

**Issued By:**
Nitin Iyer (Secretary)
Pearl Residency

**Contact:** office@pearlresidency.com
**Phone:** +91-8110875589

**Circular Number:** CIR/2025/091
**Date:** 17 November 2025
**Society Name:** Platinum Towers
**Issued By:** Managing Committee

**Subject:** Guidelines for Internal Renovation and Structural Work

**To All Members,**

This circular is issued to inform all residents about the renovation approval process that have been approved by the Managing Committee and are hereby notified for strict compliance.

**Detailed Explanation:**

To prevent unauthorized structural modifications that may compromise building safety and to minimize inconvenience to other residents, the society has formulated a comprehensive renovation approval process.

The society engineer will inspect the work site before and after renovation. Any structural damage to common areas or neighboring flats will result in forfeiture of the security deposit and additional penalty.

**Applicable Rules:**

1. NOC required from society for any structural changes
2. Refundable deposit: ₹25,000 before starting work
3. Permitted hours: 9:00 AM to 5:00 PM, Monday to Saturday
4. No work on Sundays and public holidays
5. Debris must be removed within 24 hours
6. Load-bearing walls cannot be altered under any circumstance

**Effective Date:** 25 November 2025

**Penalty for Non-Compliance:**
First Violation: Written warning
Second Violation: Fine of ₹500
Repeated Violations: Matter referred to Managing Committee for further action

**Implementation:**
- All residents must acknowledge receipt of this circular
- Tenants to be informed by flat owners
- Copy displayed on all notice boards
- Soft copy available on society app/website

For any clarifications, please contact the society office during working hours (10:00 AM to 1:00 PM and 4:00 PM to 7:00 PM).

**Circular Number:** CIR/2024/118
**Date:** 09 October 2024
**Society Name:** Victoria Apartments
**Issued By:** Secretary

**Subject:** Noise Pollution Control - Quiet Hours Policy

**To All Members,**

This circular is issued to inform all residents about the noise restrictions that have been approved by the Managing Committee and are hereby notified for strict compliance.

**Detailed Explanation:**

The society has received 39 written complaints in the last 4 months regarding noise disturbances during late hours. The Managing Committee is duty-bound to ensure peaceful living environment for all residents.

As per Noise Pollution (Regulation and Control) Rules, 2000, residential areas are classified as 'Silence Zone' and permissible noise levels must be maintained. Repeated violations may lead to police complaints.

**Applicable Rules:**

1. Quiet hours: 10:30 PM to 6:30 AM and 2:00 PM to 4:00 PM
2. No drilling, hammering, or construction noise during quiet hours
3. Music and TV volume to be kept at reasonable levels
4. Parties must end by 11:00 PM on weekdays, 12:00 AM on weekends
5. Generator usage restricted to emergencies only
6. Washing machine usage discouraged after 10:00 PM

**Effective Date:** 07 November 2024

**Penalty for Non-Compliance:**
First Violation: Written warning
Second Violation: Fine of ₹1000
Repeated Violations: Matter referred to Managing Committee for further action

**Implementation:**
- All residents must acknowledge receipt of this circular
- Tenants to be informed by flat owners
- Copy displayed on all notice boards
- Soft copy available on society app/website

For any clarifications, please contact the society office during working hours (10:00 AM to 1:00 PM and 4:00 PM to 7:00 PM).

**Issued By:**
Secretary
Victoria Apartments

**Contact:** office@victoriaapartments.com
**Phone:** +91-9409641148

**Date:** 29 June 2024
**Society Name:** Ocean View CHS
**Issued By:** Rakesh Menon (Secretary)

**Subject:** Noise Pollution Control - Quiet Hours Policy

**To All Members,**

This circular is issued to inform all residents about the noise restrictions that have been approved by the Managing Committee and are hereby notified for strict compliance.

**Detailed Explanation:**

The society has received 20 written complaints in the last 4 months regarding noise disturbances during late hours. The Managing Committee is duty-bound to ensure peaceful living environment for all residents.

As per Noise Pollution (Regulation and Control) Rules, 2000, residential areas are classified as 'Silence Zone' and permissible noise levels must be maintained. Repeated violations may lead to police complaints.

**Applicable Rules:**

1. Quiet hours: 10:30 PM to 6:30 AM and 2:00 PM to 4:00 PM
2. No drilling, hammering, or construction noise during quiet hours
3. Music and TV volume to be kept at reasonable levels
4. Parties must end by 11:00 PM on weekdays, 12:00 AM on weekends
5. Generator usage restricted to emergencies only
6. Washing machine usage discouraged after 10:00 PM

**Effective Date:** 27 July 2024

**Penalty for Non-Compliance:**
First Violation: Written warning
Second Violation: Fine of ₹1000
Repeated Violations: Matter referred to Managing Committee for further action

**Implementation:**
- All residents must acknowledge receipt of this circular
- Tenants to be informed by flat owners
- Copy displayed on all notice boards
- Soft copy available on society app/website

**Subject:** Guidelines for Internal Renovation and Structural Work

**To All Members,**

This circular is issued to inform all residents about the renovation approval process that have been approved by the Managing Committee and are hereby notified for strict compliance.

**Detailed Explanation:**

To prevent unauthorized structural modifications that may compromise building safety and to minimize inconvenience to other residents, the society has formulated a comprehensive renovation approval process.

The society engineer will inspect the work site before and after renovation. Any structural damage to common areas or neighboring flats will result in forfeiture of the security deposit and additional penalty.

**Applicable Rules:**

1. NOC required from society for any structural changes
2. Refundable deposit: ₹25,000 before starting work
3. Permitted hours: 9:00 AM to 5:00 PM, Monday to Saturday
4. No work on Sundays and public holidays
5. Debris must be removed within 24 hours
6. Load-bearing walls cannot be altered under any circumstance

**Effective Date:** 10 July 2027

**Penalty for Non-Compliance:**
First Violation: Written warning
Second Violation: Fine of ₹1000
Repeated Violations: Matter referred to Managing Committee for further action

Currently, approximately 28 pet dogs and 23 cats reside in our society. Proper registration will help maintain records and ensure responsible pet ownership.

**Applicable Rules:**

1. All pet dogs and cats must be registered with society office
2. Annual vaccination certificate to be submitted
3. Pets must be on leash (max 6 feet) in common areas
4. Pet waste must be cleaned immediately by owners
5. Aggressive pets must be muzzled in public areas
6. Lift usage allowed but give preference to seniors

**Effective Date:** 21 October 2024

**Penalty for Non-Compliance:**
First Violation: Written warning
Second Violation: Fine of ₹500
Repeated Violations: Matter referred to Managing Committee for further action

**Implementation:**
- All residents must acknowledge receipt of this circular
- Tenants to be informed by flat owners
- Copy displayed on all notice boards
- Soft copy available on society app/website

**Circular Number:** CIR/2026/123
**Date:** 10 June 2026
**Society Name:** Hillview Cooperative Housing Society
**Issued By:** Pooja Kapoor (Secretary)

**Subject:** Water Conservation Initiative - Usage Guidelines

**To All Members,**

This circular is issued to inform all residents about the water conservation measures that have been approved by the Managing Committee and are hereby notified for strict compliance.

**Detailed Explanation:**

The society's daily water consumption has increased to 69235 liters. With depleting groundwater levels and erratic municipal supply, water conservation is no longer optional but essential.

The society has installed flow meters and leak detection systems. Residents consuming excessive water (above 656 liters per day per flat) will be identified and counseled.

**Applicable Rules:**

1. Car washing limited to two buckets - no hose pipes
2. Washing timings: 6:00 AM to 10:00 AM only
3. Report leaking taps and pipes immediately
4. Rainwater harvesting system installed - cooperation required
5. Overhead tank cleaning every 3 months
6. Penalty for water wastage: ₹1,000 per incident

**Effective Date:** 21 June 2026

**Penalty for Non-Compliance:**
First Violation: Written warning
Second Violation: Fine of ₹500
Repeated Violations: Matter referred to Managing Committee for further action

**Implementation:**
- All residents must acknowledge receipt of this circular
- Tenants to be informed by flat owners
- Copy displayed on all notice boards
- Soft copy available on society app/website

For any clarifications, please contact the society office during working hours (10:00 AM to 1:00 PM and 4:00 PM to 7:00 PM).

**Date:** 01 November 2027
**Society Name:** Whispering Willows CHS
**Issued By:** Preeti Chopra (Secretary)

**Subject:** Fire Safety Measures - Mandatory Compliance Requirements

**To All Members,**

This circular is issued to inform all residents about the fire safety compliance that have been approved by the Managing Committee and are hereby notified for strict compliance.

**Detailed Explanation:**

As per Maharashtra Fire Prevention and Life Safety Measures Act, 2006, and recent directives from the Fire Department, all residential societies must comply with specified fire safety norms. The last fire audit identified 11 areas requiring immediate attention.

The society has appointed Phoenix Fire Systems as authorized fire safety consultants. Non-compliance may result in penalties from municipal authorities.

**Applicable Rules:**

1. Fire extinguishers must be recharged annually
2. Fire doors to be kept closed (not locked) at all times
3. No storage of combustible materials in staircases
4. Smoke detectors must be functional - testing every 6 months
5. Fire drill participation mandatory twice a year
6. Emergency exit routes must be unobstructed

**Effective Date:** 17 November 2027

**Penalty for Non-Compliance:**
First Violation: Written warning
Second Violation: Fine of ₹2000
Repeated Violations: Matter referred to Managing Committee for further action

**Implementation:**
- All residents must acknowledge receipt of this circular
- Tenants to be informed by flat owners
- Copy displayed on all notice boards
- Soft copy available on society app/website

**Circular Number:** CIR/2024/024
**Date:** 01 January 2024
**Society Name:** Serene Waters Society
**Issued By:** Managing Committee

**Subject:** Revised Parking Policy and Slot Allocation Guidelines

**To All Members,**

This circular is issued to inform all residents about the new parking rules that have been approved by the Managing Committee and are hereby notified for strict compliance.

**Detailed Explanation:**

The society has 199 parking slots for 293 flats. Due to increasing complaints about unauthorized parking and space shortage, the committee has revised the parking policy. This policy aims to ensure fair allocation and systematic management of limited parking resources.

The new parking rules have been formulated after consultation with 25 resident representatives and legal advisors. These rules are binding on all residents, tenants, and their guests.

**Applicable Rules:**

1. One parking slot per flat as per original allotment deed
2. Second parking available on rental basis - ₹1,500 per month
3. Guest parking limited to 6 hours - overstay fine ₹500
4. No parking in fire lane - violators will be towed
5. Visitor vehicles must display dashboard pass
6. Two-wheeler parking only in designated zones

**Effective Date:** 18 January 2024

**Penalty for Non-Compliance:**
First Violation: Written warning
Second Violation: Fine of ₹500
Repeated Violations: Matter referred to Managing Committee for further action

**Implementation:**
- All residents must acknowledge receipt of this circular
- Tenants to be informed by flat owners
- Copy displayed on all notice boards
- Soft copy available on society app/website

For any clarifications, please contact the society office during working hours (10:00 AM to 1:00 PM and 4:00 PM to 7:00 PM).

**Subject:** Guidelines for Internal Renovation and Structural Work

**To All Members,**

This circular is issued to inform all residents about the renovation approval process that have been approved by the Managing Committee and are hereby notified for strict compliance.

**Detailed Explanation:**

To prevent unauthorized structural modifications that may compromise building safety and to minimize inconvenience to other residents, the society has formulated a comprehensive renovation approval process.

The society engineer will inspect the work site before and after renovation. Any structural damage to common areas or neighboring flats will result in forfeiture of the security deposit and additional penalty.

**Applicable Rules:**

1. NOC required from society for any structural changes
2. Refundable deposit: ₹25,000 before starting work
3. Permitted hours: 9:00 AM to 5:00 PM, Monday to Saturday
4. No work on Sundays and public holidays
5. Debris must be removed within 24 hours
6. Load-bearing walls cannot be altered under any circumstance

**Effective Date:** 25 August 2024

**Penalty for Non-Compliance:**
First Violation: Written warning
Second Violation: Fine of ₹1000
Repeated Violations: Matter referred to Managing Committee for further action

**Circular Number:** CIR/2027/089
**Date:** 01 August 2027
**Society Name:** Victoria Apartments
**Issued By:** Secretary

**Subject:** Noise Pollution Control - Quiet Hours Policy

**To All Members,**

This circular is issued to inform all residents about the noise restrictions that have been approved by the Managing Committee and are hereby notified for strict compliance.

**Detailed Explanation:**

The society has received 29 written complaints in the last 6 months regarding noise disturbances during late hours. The Managing Committee is duty-bound to ensure peaceful living environment for all residents.

As per Noise Pollution (Regulation and Control) Rules, 2000, residential areas are classified as 'Silence Zone' and permissible noise levels must be maintained. Repeated violations may lead to police complaints.

**Applicable Rules:**

1. Quiet hours: 10:30 PM to 6:30 AM and 2:00 PM to 4:00 PM
2. No drilling, hammering, or construction noise during quiet hours
3. Music and TV volume to be kept at reasonable levels
4. Parties must end by 11:00 PM on weekdays, 12:00 AM on weekends
5. Generator usage restricted to emergencies only
6. Washing machine usage discouraged after 10:00 PM

**Effective Date:** 10 August 2027

**Penalty for Non-Compliance:**
First Violation: Written warning
Second Violation: Fine of ₹2000
Repeated Violations: Matter referred to Managing Committee for further action

**Implementation:**
- All residents must acknowledge receipt of this circular
- Tenants to be informed by flat owners
- Copy displayed on all notice boards
- Soft copy available on society app/website

For any clarifications, please contact the society office during working hours (10:00 AM to 1:00 PM and 4:00 PM to 7:00 PM).

**Circular Number:** CIR/2024/058

**Issued By:** Chairperson

**Subject:** Timely Payment of Maintenance Charges - Penalty Notification

**To All Members,**

This circular is issued to inform all residents about the maintenance payment reminders that have been approved by the Managing Committee and are hereby notified for strict compliance.

**Detailed Explanation:**

As of June 2024, the society has outstanding dues of ₹1,569,907 from 56 defaulting members. This affects the society's ability to maintain essential services and undertake repairs.

The committee has exhausted all informal reminders and is now compelled to take legal action. Members are urged to clear pending dues immediately to avoid legal complications and damage to their credit record.

**Applicable Rules:**

1. Maintenance due by 10th of every month
2. Late payment interest: 18% per annum after 15th
3. Defaulters beyond 60 days barred from amenities
4. Legal notice after 90 days non-payment
5. Names of chronic defaulters displayed on notice board
6. Society can initiate recovery under Section 101 of MCS Act

**Effective Date:** 22 July 2024

**Penalty for Non-Compliance:**
First Violation: Written warning
Second Violation: Fine of ₹1000
Repeated Violations: Matter referred to Managing Committee for further action

- Tenants to be informed by flat owners

**Circular Number:** CIR/2025/108
**Date:** 24 June 2025
**Society Name:** Crystal Heights
**Issued By:** Madhuri Malhotra (Secretary)

**Subject:** Noise Pollution Control - Quiet Hours Policy

**To All Members,**

This circular is issued to inform all residents about the noise restrictions that have been approved by the Managing Committee and are hereby notified for strict compliance.

**Detailed Explanation:**

The society has received 23 written complaints in the last 4 months regarding noise disturbances during late hours. The Managing Committee is duty-bound to ensure peaceful living environment for all residents.

As per Noise Pollution (Regulation and Control) Rules, 2000, residential areas are classified as 'Silence Zone' and permissible noise levels must be maintained. Repeated violations may lead to police complaints.

**Applicable Rules:**

1. Quiet hours: 10:30 PM to 6:30 AM and 2:00 PM to 4:00 PM
2. No drilling, hammering, or construction noise during quiet hours
3. Music and TV volume to be kept at reasonable levels
4. Parties must end by 11:00 PM on weekdays, 12:00 AM on weekends
5. Generator usage restricted to emergencies only
6. Washing machine usage discouraged after 10:00 PM

**Effective Date:** 14 July 2025

**Penalty for Non-Compliance:**
First Violation: Written warning
Second Violation: Fine of ₹500
Repeated Violations: Matter referred to Managing Committee for further action

**Implementation:**
- All residents must acknowledge receipt of this circular
- Tenants to be informed by flat owners
- Copy displayed on all notice boards
- Soft copy available on society app/website

**Subject:** Mandatory Registration of Pets and Animal Welfare Guidelines

**To All Members,**

This circular is issued to inform all residents about the pet registration requirement that have been approved by the Managing Committee and are hereby notified for strict compliance.

**Detailed Explanation:**

As per Animal Welfare Board of India (AWBI) guidelines and judgment of the Honorable Supreme Court, the society cannot ban pets. However, to ensure harmonious coexistence and address concerns of non-pet owners, the following registration and conduct guidelines are being implemented.

Currently, approximately 61 pet dogs and 26 cats reside in our society. Proper registration will help maintain records and ensure responsible pet ownership.

**Applicable Rules:**

1. All pet dogs and cats must be registered with society office
2. Annual vaccination certificate to be submitted
3. Pets must be on leash (max 6 feet) in common areas
4. Pet waste must be cleaned immediately by owners
5. Aggressive pets must be muzzled in public areas
6. Lift usage allowed but give preference to seniors

**Effective Date:** 10 September 2025

**Penalty for Non-Compliance:**
First Violation: Written warning
Second Violation: Fine of ₹5000
Repeated Violations: Matter referred to Managing Committee for further action

**Date:** 15 December 2027
**Society Name:** Maple Woods Enclave
**Issued By:** Estate Manager

**Subject:** Water Conservation Initiative - Usage Guidelines

**To All Members,**

This circular is issued to inform all residents about the water conservation measures that have been approved by the Managing Committee and are hereby notified for strict compliance.

**Detailed Explanation:**

The society's daily water consumption has increased to 50558 liters. With depleting groundwater levels and erratic municipal supply, water conservation is no longer optional but essential.

The society has installed flow meters and leak detection systems. Residents consuming excessive water (above 681 liters per day per flat) will be identified and counseled.

**Applicable Rules:**

1. Car washing limited to two buckets - no hose pipes
2. Washing timings: 6:00 AM to 10:00 AM only
3. Report leaking taps and pipes immediately
4. Rainwater harvesting system installed - cooperation required
5. Overhead tank cleaning every 3 months
6. Penalty for water wastage: ₹1,000 per incident

**Effective Date:** 01 January 2028

**Penalty for Non-Compliance:**
First Violation: Written warning
Second Violation: Fine of ₹500
Repeated Violations: Matter referred to Managing Committee for further action

**Implementation:**
- All residents must acknowledge receipt of this circular
- Tenants to be informed by flat owners
- Copy displayed on all notice boards
- Soft copy available on society app/website

For any clarifications, please contact the society office during working hours (10:00 AM to 1:00 PM and 4:00 PM to 7:00 PM).

**Issued By:** Managing Committee

**Subject:** Enhanced Security Protocol - Visitor Management System

**To All Members,**

This circular is issued to inform all residents about the mandatory visitor registration that have been approved by the Managing Committee and are hereby notified for strict compliance.

**Detailed Explanation:**

In view of recent security incidents in neighboring societies and to enhance the safety of all residents, the Managing Committee has decided to implement a comprehensive visitor management system. 

The society has invested ₹246,765 in digital gate management infrastructure including facial recognition cameras and automated entry systems. All residents are requested to cooperate with security personnel.

**Applicable Rules:**

1. All visitors must be pre-approved via society app
2. Manual entry allowed only after resident confirmation via intercom
3. Delivery executives restricted to lobby after 10 PM
4. Overnight guests must be registered at main gate
5. Domestic help requires police verification and photo ID
6. Frequent visitors (tutors, nurses) must have monthly pass

**Effective Date:** 11 October 2027

**Penalty for Non-Compliance:**
First Violation: Written warning
Second Violation: Fine of ₹5000
Repeated Violations: Matter referred to Managing Committee for further action

**Subject:** Guidelines for Internal Renovation and Structural Work

**To All Members,**

This circular is issued to inform all residents about the renovation approval process that have been approved by the Managing Committee and are hereby notified for strict compliance.

**Detailed Explanation:**

To prevent unauthorized structural modifications that may compromise building safety and to minimize inconvenience to other residents, the society has formulated a comprehensive renovation approval process.

The society engineer will inspect the work site before and after renovation. Any structural damage to common areas or neighboring flats will result in forfeiture of the security deposit and additional penalty.

**Applicable Rules:**

1. NOC required from society for any structural changes
2. Refundable deposit: ₹25,000 before starting work
3. Permitted hours: 9:00 AM to 5:00 PM, Monday to Saturday
4. No work on Sundays and public holidays
5. Debris must be removed within 24 hours
6. Load-bearing walls cannot be altered under any circumstance

**Subject:** Mandatory Registration of Pets and Animal Welfare Guidelines

**To All Members,**

This circular is issued to inform all residents about the pet registration requirement that have been approved by the Managing Committee and are hereby notified for strict compliance.

**Detailed Explanation:**

As per Animal Welfare Board of India (AWBI) guidelines and judgment of the Honorable Supreme Court, the society cannot ban pets. However, to ensure harmonious coexistence and address concerns of non-pet owners, the following registration and conduct guidelines are being implemented.

Currently, approximately 71 pet dogs and 11 cats reside in our society. Proper registration will help maintain records and ensure responsible pet ownership.

**Applicable Rules:**

1. All pet dogs and cats must be registered with society office
2. Annual vaccination certificate to be submitted
3. Pets must be on leash (max 6 feet) in common areas
4. Pet waste must be cleaned immediately by owners
5. Aggressive pets must be muzzled in public areas
6. Lift usage allowed but give preference to seniors

**Effective Date:** 05 November 2027

**Penalty for Non-Compliance:**
First Violation: Written warning
Second Violation: Fine of ₹1000
Repeated Violations: Matter referred to Managing Committee for further action

**Issued By:** Estate Manager

**Subject:** Water Conservation Initiative - Usage Guidelines

**To All Members,**

This circular is issued to inform all residents about the water conservation measures that have been approved by the Managing Committee and are hereby notified for strict compliance.

**Detailed Explanation:**

The society's daily water consumption has increased to 63447 liters. With depleting groundwater levels and erratic municipal supply, water conservation is no longer optional but essential.

The society has installed flow meters and leak detection systems. Residents consuming excessive water (above 666 liters per day per flat) will be identified and counseled.

**Applicable Rules:**

1. Car washing limited to two buckets - no hose pipes
2. Washing timings: 6:00 AM to 10:00 AM only
3. Report leaking taps and pipes immediately
4. Rainwater harvesting system installed - cooperation required
5. Overhead tank cleaning every 3 months
6. Penalty for water wastage: ₹1,000 per incident

**Effective Date:** 16 April 2027

**Penalty for Non-Compliance:**
First Violation: Written warning
Second Violation: Fine of ₹1000
Repeated Violations: Matter referred to Managing Committee for further action

**Implementation:**
- All residents must acknowledge receipt of this circular
- Tenants to be informed by flat owners
- Copy displayed on all notice boards

**Circular Number:** CIR/2024/086
**Date:** 02 September 2024
**Society Name:** Sunrise Enclave
**Issued By:** Managing Committee

**Subject:** Guidelines for Internal Renovation and Structural Work

**To All Members,**

This circular is issued to inform all residents about the renovation approval process that have been approved by the Managing Committee and are hereby notified for strict compliance.

**Detailed Explanation:**

To prevent unauthorized structural modifications that may compromise building safety and to minimize inconvenience to other residents, the society has formulated a comprehensive renovation approval process.

The society engineer will inspect the work site before and after renovation. Any structural damage to common areas or neighboring flats will result in forfeiture of the security deposit and additional penalty.

**Applicable Rules:**

1. NOC required from society for any structural changes
2. Refundable deposit: ₹25,000 before starting work
3. Permitted hours: 9:00 AM to 5:00 PM, Monday to Saturday
4. No work on Sundays and public holidays
5. Debris must be removed within 24 hours
6. Load-bearing walls cannot be altered under any circumstance

**Effective Date:** 24 September 2024

**Penalty for Non-Compliance:**
First Violation: Written warning
Second Violation: Fine of ₹500
Repeated Violations: Matter referred to Managing Committee for further action

**Implementation:**
- All residents must acknowledge receipt of this circular
- Tenants to be informed by flat owners
- Copy displayed on all notice boards
- Soft copy available on society app/website

**Circular Number:** CIR/2027/101
**Date:** 30 January 2027
**Society Name:** Grandeur Park Residences
**Issued By:** Chairperson

**Subject:** Noise Pollution Control - Quiet Hours Policy

**To All Members,**

This circular is issued to inform all residents about the noise restrictions that have been approved by the Managing Committee and are hereby notified for strict compliance.

**Detailed Explanation:**

The society has received 32 written complaints in the last 3 months regarding noise disturbances during late hours. The Managing Committee is duty-bound to ensure peaceful living environment for all residents.

As per Noise Pollution (Regulation and Control) Rules, 2000, residential areas are classified as 'Silence Zone' and permissible noise levels must be maintained. Repeated violations may lead to police complaints.

**Applicable Rules:**

1. Quiet hours: 10:30 PM to 6:30 AM and 2:00 PM to 4:00 PM
2. No drilling, hammering, or construction noise during quiet hours
3. Music and TV volume to be kept at reasonable levels
4. Parties must end by 11:00 PM on weekdays, 12:00 AM on weekends
5. Generator usage restricted to emergencies only
6. Washing machine usage discouraged after 10:00 PM

**Effective Date:** 16 February 2027

**Penalty for Non-Compliance:**
First Violation: Written warning
Second Violation: Fine of ₹5000
Repeated Violations: Matter referred to Managing Committee for further action

**Implementation:**
- All residents must acknowledge receipt of this circular
- Tenants to be informed by flat owners
- Copy displayed on all notice boards
- Soft copy available on society app/website

**Circular Number:** CIR/2025/120
**Date:** 24 March 2025
**Society Name:** Desert Rose Apartments
**Issued By:** Estate Manager

**Subject:** Fire Safety Measures - Mandatory Compliance Requirements

**To All Members,**

This circular is issued to inform all residents about the fire safety compliance that have been approved by the Managing Committee and are hereby notified for strict compliance.

**Detailed Explanation:**

As per Maharashtra Fire Prevention and Life Safety Measures Act, 2006, and recent directives from the Fire Department, all residential societies must comply with specified fire safety norms. The last fire audit identified 7 areas requiring immediate attention.

The society has appointed M/s Fire Safe Solutions as authorized fire safety consultants. Non-compliance may result in penalties from municipal authorities.

**Applicable Rules:**

1. Fire extinguishers must be recharged annually
2. Fire doors to be kept closed (not locked) at all times
3. No storage of combustible materials in staircases
4. Smoke detectors must be functional - testing every 6 months
5. Fire drill participation mandatory twice a year
6. Emergency exit routes must be unobstructed

**Effective Date:** 10 April 2025

**Penalty for Non-Compliance:**
First Violation: Written warning
Second Violation: Fine of ₹5000
Repeated Violations: Matter referred to Managing Committee for further action

**Implementation:**
- All residents must acknowledge receipt of this circular
- Tenants to be informed by flat owners
- Copy displayed on all notice boards
- Soft copy available on society app/website

**Circular Number:** CIR/2026/016
**Date:** 15 December 2026
**Society Name:** Spring Gardens CHS
**Issued By:** Deepika Shah (Secretary)

**Subject:** Timely Payment of Maintenance Charges - Penalty Notification

**To All Members,**

This circular is issued to inform all residents about the maintenance payment reminders that have been approved by the Managing Committee and are hereby notified for strict compliance.

**Detailed Explanation:**

As of December 2026, the society has outstanding dues of ₹2,351,654 from 33 defaulting members. This affects the society's ability to maintain essential services and undertake repairs.

The committee has exhausted all informal reminders and is now compelled to take legal action. Members are urged to clear pending dues immediately to avoid legal complications and damage to their credit record.

**Applicable Rules:**

1. Maintenance due by 10th of every month
2. Late payment interest: 18% per annum after 15th
3. Defaulters beyond 60 days barred from amenities
4. Legal notice after 90 days non-payment
5. Names of chronic defaulters displayed on notice board
6. Society can initiate recovery under Section 101 of MCS Act

**Effective Date:** 14 January 2027

**Subject:** Noise Pollution Control - Quiet Hours Policy

**To All Members,**

This circular is issued to inform all residents about the noise restrictions that have been approved by the Managing Committee and are hereby notified for strict compliance.

**Detailed Explanation:**

The society has received 29 written complaints in the last 3 months regarding noise disturbances during late hours. The Managing Committee is duty-bound to ensure peaceful living environment for all residents.

As per Noise Pollution (Regulation and Control) Rules, 2000, residential areas are classified as 'Silence Zone' and permissible noise levels must be maintained. Repeated violations may lead to police complaints.

**Applicable Rules:**

1. Quiet hours: 10:30 PM to 6:30 AM and 2:00 PM to 4:00 PM
2. No drilling, hammering, or construction noise during quiet hours
3. Music and TV volume to be kept at reasonable levels
4. Parties must end by 11:00 PM on weekdays, 12:00 AM on weekends
5. Generator usage restricted to emergencies only
6. Washing machine usage discouraged after 10:00 PM

**Effective Date:** 09 November 2027

**Penalty for Non-Compliance:**
First Violation: Written warning
Second Violation: Fine of ₹1000
Repeated Violations: Matter referred to Managing Committee for further action

**Applicable Rules:**

3. Guest parking limited to 6 hours - overstay fine ₹500
4. No parking in fire lane - violators will be towed
5. Visitor vehicles must display dashboard pass
6. Two-wheeler parking only in designated zones

**Effective Date:** 26 October 2027

**Issued By:** Estate Manager

**Subject:** Enhanced Security Protocol - Visitor Management System

**To All Members,**

This circular is issued to inform all residents about the mandatory visitor registration that have been approved by the Managing Committee and are hereby notified for strict compliance.

**Detailed Explanation:**

In view of recent security incidents in neighboring societies and to enhance the safety of all residents, the Managing Committee has decided to implement a comprehensive visitor management system. 

The society has invested ₹634,657 in digital gate management infrastructure including facial recognition cameras and automated entry systems. All residents are requested to cooperate with security personnel.

**Applicable Rules:**

1. All visitors must be pre-approved via society app
2. Manual entry allowed only after resident confirmation via intercom
3. Delivery executives restricted to lobby after 10 PM
4. Overnight guests must be registered at main gate
5. Domestic help requires police verification and photo ID
6. Frequent visitors (tutors, nurses) must have monthly pass

**Effective Date:** 08 September 2025

**Penalty for Non-Compliance:**
First Violation: Written warning
Second Violation: Fine of ₹500
Repeated Violations: Matter referred to Managing Committee for further action

**To All Members,**

This circular is issued to inform all residents about the fire safety compliance that have been approved by the Managing Committee and are hereby notified for strict compliance.

**Detailed Explanation:**

As per Maharashtra Fire Prevention and Life Safety Measures Act, 2006, and recent directives from the Fire Department, all residential societies must comply with specified fire safety norms. The last fire audit identified 12 areas requiring immediate attention.

The society has appointed SecureFire India as authorized fire safety consultants. Non-compliance may result in penalties from municipal authorities.

**Applicable Rules:**

1. Fire extinguishers must be recharged annually
2. Fire doors to be kept closed (not locked) at all times
3. No storage of combustible materials in staircases
4. Smoke detectors must be functional - testing every 6 months
5. Fire drill participation mandatory twice a year
6. Emergency exit routes must be unobstructed

**Effective Date:** 06 June 2027

**Penalty for Non-Compliance:**
First Violation: Written warning
Second Violation: Fine of ₹5000
Repeated Violations: Matter referred to Managing Committee for further action

**Society Name:** Emerald Bay CHS
**Issued By:** Secretary

**Subject:** Guidelines for Internal Renovation and Structural Work

**To All Members,**

This circular is issued to inform all residents about the renovation approval process that have been approved by the Managing Committee and are hereby notified for strict compliance.

**Detailed Explanation:**

To prevent unauthorized structural modifications that may compromise building safety and to minimize inconvenience to other residents, the society has formulated a comprehensive renovation approval process.

The society engineer will inspect the work site before and after renovation. Any structural damage to common areas or neighboring flats will result in forfeiture of the security deposit and additional penalty.

**Applicable Rules:**

1. NOC required from society for any structural changes
2. Refundable deposit: ₹25,000 before starting work
3. Permitted hours: 9:00 AM to 5:00 PM, Monday to Saturday
4. No work on Sundays and public holidays
5. Debris must be removed within 24 hours
6. Load-bearing walls cannot be altered under any circumstance

**Effective Date:** 15 September 2025

**Penalty for Non-Compliance:**
First Violation: Written warning
Second Violation: Fine of ₹5000
Repeated Violations: Matter referred to Managing Committee for further action

- Tenants to be informed by flat owners

The committee has exhausted all informal reminders and is now compelled to take legal action. Members are urged to clear pending dues immediately to avoid legal complications and damage to their credit record.

**Applicable Rules:**

1. Maintenance due by 10th of every month
2. Late payment interest: 18% per annum after 15th
3. Defaulters beyond 60 days barred from amenities
4. Legal notice after 90 days non-payment
5. Names of chronic defaulters displayed on notice board
6. Society can initiate recovery under Section 101 of MCS Act

**Effective Date:** 09 July 2026

**Penalty for Non-Compliance:**
First Violation: Written warning
Second Violation: Fine of ₹5000
Repeated Violations: Matter referred to Managing Committee for further action

**Subject:** Revised Parking Policy and Slot Allocation Guidelines

**To All Members,**

This circular is issued to inform all residents about the new parking rules that have been approved by the Managing Committee and are hereby notified for strict compliance.

**Detailed Explanation:**

The society has 112 parking slots for 184 flats. Due to increasing complaints about unauthorized parking and space shortage, the committee has revised the parking policy. This policy aims to ensure fair allocation and systematic management of limited parking resources.

The new parking rules have been formulated after consultation with 27 resident representatives and legal advisors. These rules are binding on all residents, tenants, and their guests.

**Applicable Rules:**

1. One parking slot per flat as per original allotment deed
2. Second parking available on rental basis - ₹1,500 per month
3. Guest parking limited to 6 hours - overstay fine ₹500
4. No parking in fire lane - violators will be towed
5. Visitor vehicles must display dashboard pass
6. Two-wheeler parking only in designated zones

**Effective Date:** 20 March 2025

First Violation: Written warning

**Circular Number:** CIR/2024/196
**Date:** 29 October 2024
**Society Name:** Sunrise Enclave
**Issued By:** Secretary

**Subject:** Water Conservation Initiative - Usage Guidelines

**To All Members,**

This circular is issued to inform all residents about the water conservation measures that have been approved by the Managing Committee and are hereby notified for strict compliance.

**Detailed Explanation:**

The society's daily water consumption has increased to 40265 liters. With depleting groundwater levels and erratic municipal supply, water conservation is no longer optional but essential.

The society has installed flow meters and leak detection systems. Residents consuming excessive water (above 765 liters per day per flat) will be identified and counseled.

**Applicable Rules:**

1. Car washing limited to two buckets - no hose pipes
2. Washing timings: 6:00 AM to 10:00 AM only
3. Report leaking taps and pipes immediately
4. Rainwater harvesting system installed - cooperation required
5. Overhead tank cleaning every 3 months
6. Penalty for water wastage: ₹1,000 per incident

**Effective Date:** 12 November 2024

**Penalty for Non-Compliance:**
First Violation: Written warning
Second Violation: Fine of ₹500
Repeated Violations: Matter referred to Managing Committee for further action

**Implementation:**
- All residents must acknowledge receipt of this circular
- Tenants to be informed by flat owners
- Copy displayed on all notice boards
- Soft copy available on society app/website

**Subject:** Guidelines for Internal Renovation and Structural Work

**To All Members,**

This circular is issued to inform all residents about the renovation approval process that have been approved by the Managing Committee and are hereby notified for strict compliance.

**Detailed Explanation:**

To prevent unauthorized structural modifications that may compromise building safety and to minimize inconvenience to other residents, the society has formulated a comprehensive renovation approval process.

The society engineer will inspect the work site before and after renovation. Any structural damage to common areas or neighboring flats will result in forfeiture of the security deposit and additional penalty.

**Applicable Rules:**

1. NOC required from society for any structural changes
2. Refundable deposit: ₹25,000 before starting work
3. Permitted hours: 9:00 AM to 5:00 PM, Monday to Saturday
4. No work on Sundays and public holidays
5. Debris must be removed within 24 hours
6. Load-bearing walls cannot be altered under any circumstance

**Effective Date:** 13 July 2025

**Penalty for Non-Compliance:**
First Violation: Written warning
Second Violation: Fine of ₹2000
Repeated Violations: Matter referred to Managing Committee for further action

**Circular Number:** CIR/2027/084
**Date:** 03 March 2027
**Society Name:** Pearl Residency
**Issued By:** Managing Committee

**Subject:** Fire Safety Measures - Mandatory Compliance Requirements

**To All Members,**

This circular is issued to inform all residents about the fire safety compliance that have been approved by the Managing Committee and are hereby notified for strict compliance.

**Detailed Explanation:**

As per Maharashtra Fire Prevention and Life Safety Measures Act, 2006, and recent directives from the Fire Department, all residential societies must comply with specified fire safety norms. The last fire audit identified 8 areas requiring immediate attention.

The society has appointed Phoenix Fire Systems as authorized fire safety consultants. Non-compliance may result in penalties from municipal authorities.

**Applicable Rules:**

1. Fire extinguishers must be recharged annually
2. Fire doors to be kept closed (not locked) at all times
3. No storage of combustible materials in staircases
4. Smoke detectors must be functional - testing every 6 months
5. Fire drill participation mandatory twice a year
6. Emergency exit routes must be unobstructed

**Effective Date:** 23 March 2027

**Penalty for Non-Compliance:**
First Violation: Written warning
Second Violation: Fine of ₹500
Repeated Violations: Matter referred to Managing Committee for further action

**Implementation:**
- All residents must acknowledge receipt of this circular
- Tenants to be informed by flat owners
- Copy displayed on all notice boards
- Soft copy available on society app/website

For any clarifications, please contact the society office during working hours (10:00 AM to 1:00 PM and 4:00 PM to 7:00 PM).

**Issued By:**
Managing Committee
Pearl Residency

**Contact:** office@pearlresidency.com
**Phone:** +91-9539519248
